        "import pandas as pd\n",
        "import numpy as np\n",
        "\n",
        "from rdkit import Chem\n",
        "from rdkit.Chem import Descriptors, Lipinski"
      ],
      "metadata": {
        "id": "duvOpUXUz7iS"
      },
      "execution_count": null,
      "outputs": []
    },
    {
      "cell_type": "code",
      "source": [
        "def molecular_features(smiles):\n",
        "\n",
        "    mol = Chem.MolFromSmiles(smiles)\n",
        "\n",
        "    if mol is None:\n",
        "        return None\n",
        "\n",
        "    return {\n",
        "        \"molecular_weight\": Descriptors.MolWt(mol),\n",
        "        \"logp\": Descriptors.MolLogP(mol),\n",
        "        \"h_bond_donors\": Lipinski.NumHDonors(mol),\n",
        "        \"h_bond_acceptors\": Lipinski.NumHAcceptors(mol),\n",
        "        \"rotatable_bonds\": Lipinski.NumRotatableBonds(mol),\n",
        "        \"tpsa\": Descriptors.TPSA(mol),\n",
        "        \"num_atoms\": mol.GetNumAtoms(),\n",
        "        \"num_rings\": Lipinski.RingCount(mol)\n",
        "    }"
      ],
      "metadata": {
        "id": "XP-1gp8qz-RM"
      },
      "execution_count": null,
      "outputs": []
    },
    {
      "cell_type": "code",
      "source": [
        "smiles = \"CCO\"\n",
        "\n",
        "features = molecular_features(smiles)\n",
        "\n",
        "features"
      ],
      "metadata": {

      "cell_type": "code",
      "source": [
        "molecules = {\n",
        "    \"Ethanol\": \"CCO\",\n",
        "    \"Aspirin\": \"CC(=O)OC1=CC=CC=C1C(=O)O\",\n",
        "    \"Caffeine\": \"Cn1c(=O)c2c(ncn2C)n(C)c1=O\"\n",
        "}\n",
        "\n",
        "rows = []\n",
        "\n",
        "for name, smiles in molecules.items():\n",
        "\n",
        "    features = molecular_features(smiles)\n",
        "\n",
        "    if features:\n",
        "        features[\"name\"] = name\n",
        "        features[\"smiles\"] = smiles\n",
        "        rows.append(features)\n",
        "\n",
        "df = pd.DataFrame(rows)\n",
        "\n",
        "df"

        "from rdkit import Chem\n",
        "\n",
        "def is_valid_smiles(smiles):\n",
        "    return Chem.MolFromSmiles(smiles) is not None\n",
        "\n",
        "df[\"valid_smiles\"] = df[\"smiles\"].apply(is_valid_smiles)\n",

        "print(df[\"valid_smiles\"].value_counts())"

    {
      "cell_type": "code",
      "source": [
        "from rdkit.Chem import Descriptors, Lipinski\n",
        "\n",
        "def molecular_features(smiles):\n",
        "\n",
        "    mol = Chem.MolFromSmiles(smiles)\n",
        "\n",
        "    return [\n",
        "        Descriptors.MolWt(mol),\n",
        "        Descriptors.MolLogP(mol),\n",
        "        Lipinski.NumHDonors(mol),\n",
        "        Lipinski.NumHAcceptors(mol),\n",
        "        Lipinski.NumRotatableBonds(mol),\n",
        "        Descriptors.TPSA(mol),\n",
        "        mol.GetNumAtoms(),\n",
        "        Lipinski.RingCount(mol)\n",
        "    ]\n",
        "\n",
        "feature_names = [\n",
        "    \"molecular_weight\",\n",
        "    \"logp\",\n",
        "    \"h_bond_donors\",\n",
        "    \"h_bond_acceptors\",\n",
        "    \"rotatable_bonds\",\n",
        "    \"tpsa\",\n",
        "    \"num_atoms\",\n",
        "    \"num_rings\"\n",
        "]\n",
        "\n",
        "features = df[\"smiles\"].apply(molecular_features)\n",
        "\n",
        "X = pd.DataFrame(\n",
        "    features.tolist(),\n",
        "    columns=feature_names\n",
        ")\n",
        "\n",
        "y = df[\"measured log solubility in mols per litre\"]\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"X shape:\", X.shape)\n",
        "print(\"y shape:\", y.shape)\n",
        "\n",
        "X.head()"

        "from rdkit.Chem import AllChem\n",
        "from rdkit import DataStructs\n",
        "\n",
        "def get_morgan_fingerprint(smiles, radius=2, n_bits=2048):\n",
        "    mol = Chem.MolFromSmiles(smiles)\n",
        "\n",
        "    if mol is None:\n",
        "        return None\n",
        "\n",
        "    fingerprint = AllChem.GetMorganFingerprintAsBitVect(\n",
        "        mol,\n",
        "        radius,\n",
        "        nBits=n_bits\n",
        "    )\n",
        "\n",
        "    return np.array(fingerprint)\n",
        "\n",
        "fingerprints = df[\"smiles\"].apply(get_morgan_fingerprint)\n",
        "\n",
        "X_fp = np.array(fingerprints.tolist())\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"Fingerprint shape:\", X_fp.shape)"

        "from rdkit.Chem.Scaffolds import MurckoScaffold\n",
        "\n",
        "def get_scaffold(smiles):\n",
        "    mol = Chem.MolFromSmiles(smiles)\n",
        "\n",
        "    if mol is None:\n",
        "        return None\n",
        "\n",
        "    return MurckoScaffold.MurckoScaffoldSmiles(mol=mol)\n",
        "\n",
        "df[\"scaffold\"] = df[\"smiles\"].apply(get_scaffold)\n",
        "\n",
        "print(df[[\"smiles\", \"scaffold\"]].head())"

        "\n",

        "\n",
        "model_scaffold = XGBRegressor(\n",
        "    n_estimators=600,\n",

        "    max_depth=4,\n",
        "    min_child_weight=2,\n",
        "    subsample=0.8,\n",

        "    objective=\"reg:squarederror\",\n",
        "    random_state=42,\n",
        "    n_jobs=-1\n",
        ")\n",
        "\n",
        "model_scaffold.fit(\n",
        "    X_scaffold_train,\n",
        "    y_scaffold_train\n",

      "cell_type": "code",
      "source": [
        "from rdkit import Chem\n",
        "from rdkit.Chem import Descriptors, Lipinski\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def analyze_molecule(smiles):\n",
        "\n",
        "    mol = Chem.MolFromSmiles(smiles)\n",
        "\n",
        "    if mol is None:\n",
        "        return {\n",
        "            \"valid\": False,\n",
        "            \"error\": \"Invalid SMILES\"\n",
        "        }\n",
        "\n",
        "    molecular_weight = Descriptors.MolWt(mol)\n",
        "    logp = Descriptors.MolLogP(mol)\n",
        "    hbd = Lipinski.NumHDonors(mol)\n",
        "    hba = Lipinski.NumHAcceptors(mol)\n",
        "    rotatable = Lipinski.NumRotatableBonds(mol)\n",
        "    tpsa = Descriptors.TPSA(mol)\n",
        "\n",
        "    violations = 0\n",
        "\n",
        "    if molecular_weight > 500:\n",
        "        violations += 1\n",
        "\n",
        "    if logp > 5:\n",
        "        violations += 1\n",
        "\n",
        "    if hbd > 5:\n",
        "        violations += 1\n",
        "\n",
        "    if hba > 10:\n",
        "        violations += 1\n",
        "\n",
        "    return {\n",
        "        \"valid\": True,\n",
        "        \"molecular_weight\": round(molecular_weight, 2),\n",
        "        \"logp\": round(logp, 2),\n",
        "        \"h_bond_donors\": hbd,\n",
        "        \"h_bond_acceptors\": hba,\n",
        "        \"rotatable_bonds\": rotatable,\n",
        "        \"tpsa\": round(tpsa, 2),\n",
        "        \"lipinski_violations\": violations,\n",
        "        \"drug_like\": violations <= 1\n",
        "    }"
      ],
      "metadata": {
        "id": "km_uYXyP33vU"
      },
      "execution_count": null,
      "outputs": []
    },

        "\n",
        "result = analyze_molecule(aspirin)\n",
        "\n",

        "from rdkit import DataStructs\n",
        "from rdkit.Chem import AllChem\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def get_fingerprint(smiles):\n",
        "\n",
        "    mol = Chem.MolFromSmiles(smiles)\n",
        "\n",
        "    if mol is None:\n",
        "        return None\n",
        "\n",
        "    return AllChem.GetMorganFingerprintAsBitVect(\n",
        "        mol,\n",
        "        radius=2,\n",
        "        nBits=2048\n",
        "    )"
      ],
      "metadata": {
        "id": "ss319TV34HWF"

      "cell_type": "code",
      "source": [
        "def find_similar_molecules(\n",
        "    query_smiles,\n",
        "    top_n=10\n",
        "):\n",
        "\n",
        "    query_fp = get_fingerprint(query_smiles)\n",
        "\n",
        "    if query_fp is None:\n",
        "        return None\n",
        "\n",
        "    similarities = []\n",
        "\n",
        "    for i, fp in enumerate(dataset_fingerprints):\n",
        "\n",
        "        similarity = DataStructs.TanimotoSimilarity(\n",
        "            query_fp,\n",
        "            fp\n",
        "        )\n",
        "\n",
        "        similarities.append({\n",
        "            \"index\": i,\n",
        "            \"similarity\": similarity\n",
        "        })\n",
        "\n",
        "    similarities = sorted(\n",
        "        similarities,\n",
        "        key=lambda x: x[\"similarity\"],\n",
        "        reverse=True\n",
        "    )\n",
        "\n",
        "    results = []\n",
        "\n",
        "    for item in similarities[:top_n]:\n",
        "\n",
        "        idx = item[\"index\"]\n",
        "\n",
        "        results.append({\n",
        "            \"compound_id\": df.iloc[idx][\"Compound ID\"],\n",
        "            \"smiles\": df.iloc[idx][\"smiles\"],\n",
        "            \"similarity\": round(\n",
        "                item[\"similarity\"],\n",
        "                4\n",
        "            ),\n",
        "            \"measured_logS\": df.iloc[idx][\n",
        "                \"measured log solubility in mols per litre\"\n",
        "            ]\n",
        "        })\n",
        "\n",
        "    return pd.DataFrame(results)"
      ],
      "metadata": {
        "id": "D93tdHlB4TF7"
      },
      "execution_count": null,
      "outputs": []
    },

      "cell_type": "code",
      "source": [
        "def prepare_molecule_features(smiles):\n",
        "\n",
        "    mol = Chem.MolFromSmiles(smiles)\n",
        "\n",
        "    if mol is None:\n",
        "        return None\n",
        "\n",
        "    # RDKit descriptors\n",
        "    descriptors = [\n",
        "        Descriptors.MolWt(mol),\n",
        "        Descriptors.MolLogP(mol),\n",
        "        Lipinski.NumHDonors(mol),\n",
        "        Lipinski.NumHAcceptors(mol),\n",
        "        Lipinski.NumRotatableBonds(mol),\n",
        "        Descriptors.TPSA(mol),\n",
        "        mol.GetNumAtoms(),\n",
        "        Lipinski.RingCount(mol)\n",
        "    ]\n",
        "\n",
        "    # Morgan fingerprint\n",
        "    fingerprint = AllChem.GetMorganFingerprintAsBitVect(\n",
        "        mol,\n",
        "        radius=2,\n",
        "        nBits=2048\n",
        "    )\n",
        "\n",
        "    fingerprint_array = np.array(fingerprint)\n",
        "\n",
        "    # Combine\n",
        "    combined = np.concatenate([\n",
        "        descriptors,\n",
        "        fingerprint_array\n",
        "    ])\n",
        "\n",
        "    return combined"
      ],
      "metadata": {
        "id": "HvbuJaW24aai"
      },
      "execution_count": null,
      "outputs": []
    },
    {
      "cell_type": "code",
      "source": [
        "def predict_solubility(smiles):\n",
        "\n",
        "    features = prepare_molecule_features(smiles)\n",
        "\n",
        "    if features is None:\n",
        "        return None\n",
        "\n",
        "    features = features.reshape(1, -1)\n",
        "\n",
        "    prediction = xgb_combined.predict(\n",
        "        features\n",
        "    )[0]\n",
        "\n",
        "    return float(prediction)"
      ],
      "metadata": {
        "id": "EG4YoGNL4je7"
      },
      "execution_count": null,
      "outputs": []
    },
    {
      "cell_type": "code",
      "source": [

        "\n",
        "prediction = predict_solubility(\n",
        "    aspirin\n",

    {
      "cell_type": "code",
      "source": [
        "def virtual_screening(\n",
        "    molecules,\n",
        "    top_n=20\n",
        "):\n",
        "\n",
        "    results = []\n",
        "\n",
        "    for molecule_id, smiles in molecules.items():\n",
        "\n",
        "        features = prepare_molecule_features(\n",
        "            smiles\n",
        "        )\n",
        "\n",
        "        if features is None:\n",
        "            continue\n",
        "\n",
        "        prediction = xgb_combined.predict(\n",
        "            features.reshape(1, -1)\n",
        "        )[0]\n",
        "\n",
        "        mol = Chem.MolFromSmiles(smiles)\n",
        "\n",
        "        results.append({\n",
        "            \"molecule_id\": molecule_id,\n",
        "            \"smiles\": smiles,\n",
        "            \"predicted_logS\": float(prediction),\n",
        "            \"molecular_weight\": Descriptors.MolWt(mol),\n",
        "            \"logp\": Descriptors.MolLogP(mol),\n",
        "            \"tpsa\": Descriptors.TPSA(mol),\n",
        "            \"hbd\": Lipinski.NumHDonors(mol),\n",
        "            \"hba\": Lipinski.NumHAcceptors(mol)\n",
        "        })\n",
        "\n",
        "    results = pd.DataFrame(results)\n",
        "\n",
        "    # Higher LogS = more soluble\n",
        "    results = results.sort_values(\n",
        "        \"predicted_logS\",\n",
        "        ascending=False\n",
        "    )\n",
        "\n",
        "    return results.head(top_n).reset_index(\n",
        "        drop=True\n",
        "    )"
      ],
      "metadata": {
        "id": "FWjlMgLX4pd7"
      },
      "execution_count": null,
      "outputs": []
    },

    {
      "cell_type": "code",
      "source": [
        "def molecule_analyzer(smiles, top_n=5):\n",
        "\n",
        "    # -----------------------------------------\n",
        "    # 1. Validate molecule\n",
        "    # -----------------------------------------\n",
        "\n",
        "    mol = Chem.MolFromSmiles(smiles)\n",
        "\n",
        "    if mol is None:\n",
        "        return {\n",
        "            \"valid\": False,\n",
        "            \"error\": \"Invalid SMILES\"\n",
        "        }\n",
        "\n",
        "    # -----------------------------------------\n",
        "    # 2. Molecular properties\n",
        "    # -----------------------------------------\n",
        "\n",
        "    molecular_weight = Descriptors.MolWt(mol)\n",
        "    logp = Descriptors.MolLogP(mol)\n",
        "    hbd = Lipinski.NumHDonors(mol)\n",
        "    hba = Lipinski.NumHAcceptors(mol)\n",
        "    rotatable = Lipinski.NumRotatableBonds(mol)\n",
        "    tpsa = Descriptors.TPSA(mol)\n",
        "    atoms = mol.GetNumAtoms()\n",
        "    rings = Lipinski.RingCount(mol)\n",
        "\n",
        "    # -----------------------------------------\n",
        "    # 3. Lipinski violations\n",
        "    # -----------------------------------------\n",
        "\n",
        "    violations = 0\n",
        "\n",
        "    if molecular_weight > 500:\n",
        "        violations += 1\n",
        "\n",
        "    if logp > 5:\n",
        "        violations += 1\n",
        "\n",
        "    if hbd > 5:\n",
        "        violations += 1\n",
        "\n",
        "    if hba > 10:\n",
        "        violations += 1\n",
        "\n",
        "    # -----------------------------------------\n",
        "    # 4. ML prediction\n",
        "    # -----------------------------------------\n",
        "\n",
        "    features = prepare_molecule_features(smiles)\n",
        "\n",
        "    prediction = xgb_combined.predict(\n",
        "        features.reshape(1, -1)\n",
        "    )[0]\n",
        "\n",
        "    # -----------------------------------------\n",
        "    # 5. Similarity search\n",
        "    # -----------------------------------------\n",
        "\n",
        "    similar = find_similar_molecules(\n",
        "        smiles,\n",
        "        top_n=top_n\n",
        "    )\n",
        "\n",
        "    # -----------------------------------------\n",
        "    # 6. Final result\n",
        "    # -----------------------------------------\n",
        "\n",
        "    return {\n",
        "        \"valid\": True,\n",
        "\n",
        "        \"molecular_properties\": {\n",
        "            \"molecular_weight\": round(\n",
        "                molecular_weight, 2\n",
        "            ),\n",
        "            \"logp\": round(\n",
        "                logp, 2\n",
        "            ),\n",
        "            \"h_bond_donors\": hbd,\n",
        "            \"h_bond_acceptors\": hba,\n",
        "            \"rotatable_bonds\": rotatable,\n",
        "            \"tpsa\": round(\n",
        "                tpsa, 2\n",
        "            ),\n",
        "            \"num_atoms\": atoms,\n",
        "            \"num_rings\": rings\n",
        "        },\n",
        "\n",
        "        \"lipinski\": {\n",
        "            \"violations\": violations,\n",
        "            \"drug_like\": violations <= 1\n",
        "        },\n",
        "\n",
        "        \"predicted_logS\": round(\n",
        "            float(prediction), 4\n",
        "        ),\n",
        "\n",
        "        \"similar_molecules\": similar\n",
        "    }"
      ],
      "metadata": {
        "id": "0_Yi6hdK40aW"
      },
      "execution_count": null,
      "outputs": []
    },

              "\n",
              "    async function download(id, filename, size) {\n",
              "      if (!google.colab.kernel.accessAllowed) {\n",
              "        return;\n",
              "      }\n",
              "      const div = document.createElement('div');\n",
              "      const label = document.createElement('label');\n",
              "      label.textContent = `Downloading \"${filename}\": `;\n",
              "      div.appendChild(label);\n",
              "      const progress = document.createElement('progress');\n",
              "      progress.max = size;\n",
              "      div.appendChild(progress);\n",
              "      document.body.appendChild(div);\n",
              "\n",
              "      const buffers = [];\n",
              "      let downloaded = 0;\n",
              "\n",
              "      const channel = await google.colab.kernel.comms.open(id);\n",
              "      // Send a message to notify the kernel that we're ready.\n",
              "      channel.send({})\n",
              "\n",
              "      for await (const message of channel.messages) {\n",
              "        // Send a message to notify the kernel that we're ready.\n",
              "        channel.send({})\n",
              "        if (message.buffers) {\n",
              "          for (const buffer of message.buffers) {\n",
              "            buffers.push(buffer);\n",
              "            downloaded += buffer.byteLength;\n",
              "            progress.value = downloaded;\n",
              "          }\n",
              "        }\n",
              "      }\n",
              "      const blob = new Blob(buffers, {type: 'application/binary'});\n",
              "      const a = document.createElement('a');\n",
              "      a.href = window.URL.createObjectURL(blob);\n",
              "      a.download = filename;\n",
              "      div.appendChild(a);\n",
              "      a.click();\n",
              "      div.remove();\n",
              "    }\n",
              "  "
            ]
          },
          "metadata": {}
        },
        {
          "output_type": "display_data",
          "data": {
            "text/plain": [
              "<IPython.core.display.Javascript object>"
            ],

            ]
          },

              "\n",

              "      channel.send({})\n",
              "\n",
              "      for await (const message of channel.messages) {\n",
              "        // Send a message to notify the kernel that we're ready.\n",
              "        channel.send({})\n",
              "        if (message.buffers) {\n",
              "          for (const buffer of message.buffers) {\n",
              "            buffers.push(buffer);\n",
              "            downloaded += buffer.byteLength;\n",
              "            progress.value = downloaded;\n",
              "          }\n",
